I successfully modified the required files:\n\n**Files changed:**\n1. **`/hada/task_agent.py`** — Enhanced the prompt to enforce structural L-SHADE improvements:\n   - Added **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS** requiring grep verification against forbidden per-individual Python loops and confirming LPSR presence before submission.\n   - Added **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN** with a NumPy example to avoid timeout-causing per-individual loops.\n   - Updated **STRICT REJECTION LIST** to explicitly reject LHS initialization and unwired controller classes (based on gen_74\'s regression from 0.93121 → 0.8846).\n   - Retained the reference to the proven gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff.\n\n2. **`/hada/hyper_agent.py`** — Added a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section so future meta-agents:\n   - Know that LHS/quasi-random initialization hurt performance.\n   - Avoid adding controller classes without wiring them into `meta_learning.py`.\n   - Enforce vectorized implementation checks to prevent timeout failures.\n\nBoth files were verified after editing.'

Output: 'I have completed my task. The required modifications have been applied to both `/hada/task_agent.py` and `/hada/hyper_agent.py`.\n\n**Summary of changes:**\n\n1. **`/hada/task_agent.py`** — The prompt now includes:\n   - **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS**: Requires the Task Agent to run grep for forbidden per-individual Python loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`) and to confirm LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present before submitting.\n   - **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN**: Provides a NumPy example to prevent timeout-causing per-individual loops (the #1 recurring failure, gen_62 scored 0.8549).\n   - **UPDATED STRICT REJECTION LIST**: Explicitly rejects LHS/quasi-random initialization and unwired controller classes based on gen_74\'s regression to 0.8846 (below the best 0.93121).\n   - **Retained the reference** to the proven gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff (score 0.93121).\n\n2. **`/hada/hyper_agent.py`** — Added a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section so future meta-agents:\n   - Know that LHS initialization hurt performance (0.8846 < 0.93121).\n   - Avoid adding controller classes unless wired into `meta_learning.py` (gen_74 added `LSHADEHeuristicController` as dead code).\n   - Enforce vectorized implementation checks and always inspect the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`.'

Output: 'The task is complete. Here is a final summary of what was accomplished:\n\n## Changes Made\n\n### 1. `/hada/task_agent.py`\nAdded three critical sections to the system prompt:\n\n- **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS**: Requires the Task Agent to run grep commands verifying:\n  - No forbidden per-individual loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`)\n  - LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present\n  - `DEOptimizer` class exists\n\n- **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN**: Provides a NumPy example for vectorized current-to-pbest/1 mutation to prevent timeout-causing Python loops (gen_62 scored 0.8549 due to this failure).\n\n- **STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED)**: Explicitly rejects:\n  - LHS/quasi-random initialization (gen_74 scored 0.8846, below best 0.93121)\n  - Unwired controller classes (gen_74 added `LSHADEHeuristicController` but never trained it)\n  - Parameter tweaks to F/CR/pop_size values\n\nAlso retained the reference to the proven gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff (score 0.93121).\n\n### 2. `/hada/hyper_agent.py`\nAdded a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section so future meta-agents know:\n- LHS initialization hurt performance (0.8846 < 0.93121)\n- New controller classes without wiring into `meta_learning.py` are useless\n- Per-individual Python loops cause timeouts\n- Always check the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`\n\nBoth files were verified after editing. No further action is needed.'

Output: 'The task is complete. Both `/hada/task_agent.py` and `/hada/hyper_agent.py` have been modified and verified.\n\n**Summary of changes:**\n\n1. **`/hada/task_agent.py`** — Enhanced the Task Agent system prompt with:\n   - **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS**: Requires grep verification against forbidden per-individual Python loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`) and confirmation that LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present before submission.\n   - **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN**: Provides a NumPy example to prevent timeout-causing per-individual loops (a known recurring failure).\n   - **STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED)**: Explicitly rejects LHS/quasi-random initialization and unwired controller classes based on gen_74 regression (score 0.8846, below best 0.93121).\n   - **Retained reference** to the proven gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff at `/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_11/bbob_unconstrained_eval/agent_evals/all_patch.diff`.\n\n2. **`/hada/hyper_agent.py`** — Added a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section so future meta-agents know:\n   - LHS initialization hurt performance.\n   - New controller classes without wiring into `meta_learning.py` are useless.\n   - Per-individual Python loops cause timeouts.\n   - Always check the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`.\n\nAll modifications have been applied and verified. No further action is required.'

Output: 'All requested modifications have been completed and verified. Here is the final summary:\n\n## Changes Made\n\n### `/hada/task_agent.py`\nThe Task Agent system prompt now includes:\n1. **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS** – Requires running grep commands to verify:\n   - No forbidden per-individual loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`)\n   - LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present\n   - `DEOptimizer` class exists\n\n2. **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN** – Provides a NumPy example for vectorized mutation to prevent timeout-causing Python loops (the #1 recurring failure, gen_62 scored 0.8549).\n\n3. **STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED)** – Explicitly rejects:\n   - LHS/quasi-random initialization (gen_74 scored 0.8846, below the best 0.93121)\n   - Adding controller classes without wiring them into `meta_learning.py`\n   - Parameter tweaks to F/CR/pop_size values\n\n4. **Retained the reference** to the proven **gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff** at `/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_11/bbob_unconstrained_eval/agent_evals/all_patch.diff`.\n\n### `/hada/hyper_agent.py`\nAdded a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section so future meta-agents:\n- Know LHS initialization hurt performance (0.8846 vs 0.93121)\n- Avoid adding dead controller classes (unwired `LSHADEHeuristicController`)\n- Enforce vectorized implementation checks\n- Always inspect the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`\n\n**Both files were edited, verified, and are ready for the next generation.** No further action is needed.'

Output: 'Understood. The task is complete. Here is the final concise summary:\n\n## Completed Changes\n\n**1. Modified `/hada/task_agent.py`** — Enhanced the Task Agent system prompt with:\n- **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS**: Requires grep verification to reject forbidden per-individual Python loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`) and ensure LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present before submission.\n- **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN**: Provides a NumPy example for vectorized mutation to prevent timeout-causing Python loops (the #1 recurring failure).\n- **STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED)**: Explicitly rejects LHS/quasi-random initialization and unwired controller classes based on gen_74 regression (0.8846 vs best 0.93121).\n- **Retained reference** to the proven gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff at `/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_11/bbob_unconstrained_eval/agent_evals/all_patch.diff`.\n\n**2. Modified `/hada/hyper_agent.py`** — Added a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section for future meta-agents, documenting:\n- LHS initialization hurt performance (0.8846 vs 0.93121).\n- New controller classes without wiring into `meta_learning.py` are useless (dead code).\n- Per-individual Python loops cause timeouts.\n- Always check the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`.\n\nBoth files were edited, verified, and are ready for the next generation. No further action is required.'

Do not submit code that contains these loops.\n   208\t\n   209\tIf you implement any JADE/SHADE-style algorithm and `LPSR`/`new_pop_size`/`pop_min` is not present, **STOP and add Linear Population Size Reduction** — SHADE/JADE without LPSR is a known non-improvement (gen_29=0.8875, gen_62=0.8549).\n   210\t\n   211\t## VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN (USE THIS TO AVOID TIMEOUTS)\n   212\t\n   213\tIf implementing JADE/SHADE-style mutation, DO NOT write per-individual Python loops. Instead, generate all mutant vectors as a NumPy array:\n   214\t\n   215\t```python\n   216\t# Example: vectorized current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   217\t# pbest_idx: array of indices of pbest individuals for each i\n   218\t# r1, r2: arrays of random distinct indices\n   219\t# F_i: array of F values per individual\n   220\t# pop: current population array (n x d)\n   221\tmutants = pop[pbest_idx] + F_i[:, None] * (pop[r1] - pop[r2] + pop[archive_idx] - pop[r1])\n   222\t```\n   223\t\n   224\tUse fully vectorized NumPy operations for mutation, crossover, and selection. This is mandatory for performance - per-individual Python loops time out (gen_62 scored 0.8549 and timed out on many instances).\n   225\t\n   226\t## STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED):\n   227\t- ❌ **DO NOT add LHS or quasi-random initialization** — gen_74 added LHS and scored 0.8846, below best 0.93121. Uniform random init is sufficient.\n   228\t- ❌ **DO NOT add new controller classes unless you are also modifying meta_learning.py to train them** — gen_74 added LSHADEHeuristicController but did not wire it into training, wasting effort.\n   229\t- ❌ **DO NOT make parameter tweaks to existing F/CR/pop_size values** — these are non-improvements.\n   230\t- ❌ **DO NOT modify the `optimize()` signature or return contract.**\n   231\t- ❌ **DO NOT modify any file under /hada/domains/**\n   232\t\n   233\t\n   234\tNow here is the specific task input:\n   235\t"""\n"\n    }\n    </json>'

Both required files were modified and verified.\n\n## Completed Changes\n\n### `/hada/task_agent.py`\nEnhanced the Task Agent system prompt to enforce structural algorithm improvements:\n- **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS** — Requires grep verification to reject forbidden per-individual Python loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`) and confirm LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present before submission.\n- **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN** — Provides a NumPy example for vectorized current-to-pbest/1 mutation to prevent timeout-causing loops.\n- **STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED)** — Explicitly rejects:\n  - LHS/quasi-random initialization (gen_74 scored 0.8846, below best 0.93121)\n  - Adding controller classes without wiring them into `meta_learning.py`\n  - Parameter tweaks to F/CR/pop_size values\n- **Retained reference** to the proven gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff at `/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_11/bbob_unconstrained_eval/agent_evals/all_patch.diff`.\n\n### `/hada/hyper_agent.py`\nAdded a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section to guide future meta-agents:\n- LHS initialization hurt performance (0.8846 vs 0.93121).\n- New controller classes without wiring into `meta_learning.py` are dead code.\n- Per-individual Python loops cause timeouts.\n- Always inspect the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`.\n\nBoth files were viewed after editing, confirming the changes are in place with correct placement. No further action is required.'

Output: 'All required modifications have been completed and verified. Here is the final summary:\n\n## Completed Changes\n\n### 1. `/hada/task_agent.py`\nEnhanced the Task Agent system prompt to enforce structural algorithm improvements:\n- **MANDATORY STATIC CHECKS** — Requires grep verification to reject forbidden per-individual Python loops (`for i in range(pop_size)`, `for j in range(self.dim)`) and confirm LPSR (Linear Population Size Reduction) is present before submission.\n- **VECTORIZED IMPLEMENTATION PATTERN** — Provides a NumPy example for vectorized current-to-pbest/1 mutation to prevent timeout-causing loops (the #1 recurring failure).\n- **STRICT REJECTION LIST (UPDATED)** — Explicitly rejects:\n  - LHS/quasi-random initialization (gen_74 scored 0.8846, below the best 0.93121)\n  - Adding controller classes without wiring them into `meta_learning.py` (gen_74 added `LSHADEHeuristicController` as dead code)\n  - Parameter tweaks to F/CR/pop_size values\n- **Retained reference** to the proven **gen_11 L-SHADE winning diff** at `/hada/outputs/generate_20260828_142858_379759/gen_11/bbob_unconstrained_eval/agent_evals/all_patch.diff`.\n\n### 2. `/hada/hyper_agent.py`\nAdded a **"LATEST LESSONS FROM GEN_74"** section to guide future meta-agents:\n- LHS initialization hurt performance (0.8846 vs 0.93121).\n- New controller classes without wiring into `meta_learning.py` are useless.\n- Per-individual Python loops cause timeouts.\n- Always inspect the latest generation\'s diff before modifying `task_agent.py`.\n\nBoth files were viewed after editing, confirming the changes are in place with correct placement. **No further action is needed.**'

Instead, generate all mutant vectors as a NumPy array:\n   214\t\n   215\t```python\n   216\t# Example: vectorized current-to-pbest/1 mutation\n   217\t# pbest_idx: array of indices of pbest individuals for each i\n   218\t# r1, r2: arrays of random distinct indices\n   219\t# F_i: array of F values per individual\n   220\t# pop: current population array (n x d)\n   221\tmutants = pop[pbest_idx] + F_i[:, None] * (pop[r1] - pop[r2] + pop[archive_idx] - pop[r1])\n   222\t```\n   223\t\n   224\tUse fully vectorized NumPy operations for mutation, crossover, and selection. Uniform random init is sufficient.\n   228\t- ❌ **DO NOT add new controller classes unless you are also modifying meta_learning.py to train them** — gen_74 added LSHADEHeuristicController but did not wire it into training, wasting effort.\n   229\t- ❌ **DO NOT make parameter tweaks to existing F/CR/pop_size values** — these are non-improvements.\n   230\t- ❌ **DO NOT modify the `optimize()` signature or return contract.**\n   231\t- ❌ **DO NOT modify any file under /hada/domains/**\n   232\t\n"\n    }\n    </json>'
